rested.\n- key: S028\n  name: Psychomotor_Changes\n  description: Observable increase or decrease in psychomotor activity.\n  manifestation: My husband says I went from pacing through the house and moving decorations around all day to walking so slowly that it takes me forever just to start the laundry.\n- key: S029\n  name: Fatigue\n  description: Persistent fatigue that is not relieved by rest and a marked lack of energy.\n  manifestation: Even after I sit in my chair most of the morning, my body feels worn out like I spent the whole day cooking for the festival.\n- key: S030\n  name: Worthlessness_Guilt\n  description: Feelings of worthlessness or excessive or inappropriate guilt (which may be delusional; not merely self-reproach or guilt about being sick).\n  manifestation: I keep thinking I ruined everything for my family and the parish, and sometimes I feel like even the little things that went wrong are my punishment.\n- key: S033\n  name: Elevated_Irritable_Mood\n  description: A distinct period of abnormally and persistently elevated, expansive, or irritable mood.\n  manifestation: There were days when I felt so bright and certain, then if anyone questioned me about the altar I would snap at them like they were insulting my whole family.\n- key: S034\n  name: Increased_Activity\n  description: Abnormally and persistently increased goal-directed activity or energy.\n  manifestation: I could not stay still and kept cleaning, calling people, rewriting menus, and moving boxes of decorations even when everyone told me to rest.\n- key: S035\n  name: Inflated_Self_Esteem\n  description: Inflated self-esteem or grandiosity (may range from uncritical self-confidence to delusional proportions).\n  manifestation: I truly believed I was the only one who could save our family's traditions and that if people just listened to me the whole community event would be perfect.\n- key: S037\n  name: Pressured_Speech\n  description: More talkative than usual or pressure to keep talking.\n  manifestation: My daughter told me I was talking so fast about plans and family history that she could not get a word in, but I felt like I had to keep explaining before the ideas disappeared.\n- key: S039\n  name: Manic_Distractibility\n  description: Attention is too easily drawn to unimportant or irrelevant external stimuli.\n  manifestation: When I tried to work on one list, every church bell, phone buzz, or neighbor walking by would pull me away and I would start something else.\n- key: S040\n  name: Increased_Goal_Activity\n  description: Increase in goal-directed activity (at work, school, socially, or sexually) or purposeless psychomotor agitation.\n  manifestation: I signed up for extra cooking shifts, tried to organize new volunteers, and called relatives I had not spoken to in years because I felt I had to bring everyone together right away.\n- key: additional_requirement_1\n  name: Additional Requirement 1\n  description: delusions_and_hallucinations occur exclusively during the manic episode\n  manifestation: The voices and seeing my mother only happened during that time when I was barely sleeping, rushing around, and feeling sure I had a special duty to fix the altar. When my mood dropped, I still felt guilty and sad, but I was not hearing or seeing those things anymore.\n- key: functional_impairment\n  name: Functional Impairment\n  manifestation: I stopped being able to keep up with the parish preparations or even manage the house the way I usually do, and my family had to take over the festival tasks. My husband started staying close because I was missing meals, forgetting routines, and not caring for myself reliably.\n- key: duration\n  name: Duration\n  description: 2 months\n  manifestation: This whole crisis has been going on for about two months, since the fight with my in-laws over the Día de los Muertos altar.\n- key: depressive_episode_duration\n  name: Depressive Episode Duration\n  description: 9 months\n  manifestation: The heavy sadness and guilt have been with me for about nine months, though they became much harder to handle after the family conflict.\n\n[Patient Info]\n- demographics: Female / 60 / not_in_workforce / high_school\n- chief_complaint_symptom_code: S019\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Even though there’s no clear proof, like a letter or direct statement, do you still feel completely certain that your in-laws are using the church against you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I am completely certain. My husband was there and he said no one was there, but... well, I know I saw her.\n\nDoctor: How often have you heard your late mother's voice or seen her, and have these experiences happened at times when you knew you weren't dreaming or sleepy?\n\nPatient: Well, I'd say I heard her voice maybe three or four times, and I saw her just that one time. I felt so sure I had this... this special duty to fix the altar and make it right. \n\nLater on, when my mood completely dropped and I just felt so heavy and guilty and sad... I wasn't hearing or seeing her anymore.
